# HANDOVER — fully agnostic passive receiver: fix + validate everything (live file)

Location of this file: **sens6:/home/sens/NICOLA/adaptive-rx-UL-DL/HANDOVER_AGNOSTICITY.md** (untracked; the
single source of truth for status). Every agent that works on this goal MUST update the "Status log" and the
lane table whenever something is found, fixed, or validated — before moving on.

Last updated: 2026-09-27 (Claude Code coordinator).

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
## 0. Goal and deadline

All implemented features of the agnostic passive receiver (`nr-uesoftmodem --passive-rx`, blind dedicated
decoding: dedicated CORESET/SS discovery, C-RNTI recovery, DCI 1_1/0_1 layout resolution, Technique D PDSCH
config sweep, PDSCH/PUSCH decode, scrambling/DM-RS/rank/MCS-table discovery) AND every functional gap below
must be **fixed and validated** by the morning of 2026-09-28. **Technique D must converge FAST and reliably**: CONVERGED in >= 4/5 runs AND median time-to-converge <= 60 s after the first C-RNTI accept, validated live (OAI phy-test and OCUDU). Channel/clock realism does NOT matter: ideal
simulated channels are fine. Use the **OCUDU gNB** wherever it can emit the feature; the OAI gNB otherwise.

**Out of this goal:** the sensing pipeline (`openair1/PHY/NR_UE_ISAC/`, range-Doppler/CFAR/tracking) — do not
work on it. X410 / over-the-air (Task 17) — hardware unavailable. rank>4, FR2/ECP, DSS, SPS/CG, slot
aggregation, NR-U/multi-TRP, GPU solver.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
## 1. HARD RULES (every agent — Claude, Codex, or other)

1. **Hosts: ONLY sens6 and sensnuc3.** Never touch sens4, the X410, `gnb_remote_logs/`, `cuLogs/`.
   - sens6 code: `/home/sens/NICOLA/adaptive-rx-UL-DL` (branch `adaptive-rx-UL-DL`) + lane worktrees
     `/home/sens/NICOLA/agn-wt/*`. sens6 has NO 5G core → only OAI `--phy-test` runs there.
   - sensnuc3 (local host of the operator): open5gs core (AMF 127.0.0.1:38412, PLMN 001/06, mongodb), OCUDU
     gNB + srsUE in `/home/sens/NICOLA/repos/`, OAI SA trees `/home/sens/NICOLA/rfsim-local` (test gNB) and
     `/home/sens/NICOLA/rfsim-val` (UE under test). **Never use `/home/sens/NICOLA/adaptive-rx-UL-DL` on
     sensnuc3** (stale copy of someone else's work).
2. **Non-interference — ownership lock.** Exactly one coordinator owns the work at a time. The lock is
   `sensnuc3:/home/sens/NICOLA/AGENT_OWNER` (one line: `<owner> <ISO-time> <note>`).
   - Before ANY action: read it. If it names someone else and was touched < 45 min ago, do NOT start, kill,
     build, commit, or edit anything — read-only only. Claude Code obeys this too: once Codex owns, Claude
     stands down.
   - On takeover: verify quiescence first (section 5), then write your own line, and refresh its timestamp at
     least every 30 min while you work.
   - Lanes: one agent per lane worktree. Never commit in a lane you don't own (lane table, section 3).
3. **One radio harness per host at a time.** Before launching anything radio-related:
   `pgrep -x nr-uesoftmodem; pgrep -x nr-softmodem; pgrep -x gnb; pgrep -x srsue; pgrep -af run_passive_rx`
   on that host. If anything runs, wait (poll in the background) — never kill it unless you started it.
4. **Never build during a capture, never capture during a build** (sens6: before a capture, `pgrep -x make/cc1/cc1plus/ninja` empty and load avg < 8; record load at capture start; overlapping captures are VOID) on the same host (starves the UEs and silently corrupts the run). On sens6
   always build via `/home/sens/NICOLA/agn-wt/lane-make.sh <lane> <targets>` (waits for captures).
5. **Never `git stash`** (the stash stack is shared by all worktrees). Edit only files under YOUR lane worktree (absolute paths).
5b. **Kill only your own processes**, by PID or `pgrep -x`. **Never `pkill -f`** (matches your own ssh argv).
   Never `systemctl` on open5gs (a crash-looping amfd unit exists; an orphan core serves NGAP — leave it).
6. **Stale-plugin trap:** `librfsimulator.so`, `liboai_usrpdevif.so`, `oai_zmqdevif` are dlopen'd modules —
   rebuild them explicitly (`ninja rfsimulator oai_zmqdevif` / `make rfsimulator ...`).
7. **Agnosticity:** the receiver must discover every RRC-dedicated parameter blindly. gNB configs/logs are
   ground truth for VALIDATION ONLY — never seed the receiver from them. OCUDU: never set a negative
   `al_cqi_offset` (crashes the gNB) — use `ss2_n_candidates`.
8. **No push** to any remote unless the operator asks. Commit only on your lane branch.
9. **Report everything**: every experiment, finding, fix, and verdict goes in the Status log below with
   numbers, paths, and commit SHAs. Retract wrong claims explicitly; never quote an inherited number without
   re-measuring it in the configuration under test.

10. **Agent dispatch policy (orchestrator):** dispatch multiple sub-agents in parallel for independent tasks (one worktree per agent). Simple tasks (log reading, scoring, small build fixes, small reviews, handover updates) → light and fast agents/models. Use **astra** only for implementing code, orchestrating, or when debugging takes too long for a light agent. Long unattended runs = detached scripts with logs.

---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
## 2. Validation gates — ALL must pass before a commit is merged into `sdd/validation`

- **G1 Build:** `nr-uesoftmodem` + needed plugins + all test binaries build clean in the lane; no new warnings
  in touched files.
- **G2 Offline tests:** `ctest` green for `test_nr_pdcch_blind_monitor`, `test_nr_pdsch_config_sweep`,
  `test_nr_pdsch_prb_set`, `test_nr_scrambling_id_sweep` (and any new suite), also with `--gtest_shuffle`.
  Known pre-existing skips: blind_monitor 2, config_sweep 1 — anything more is a failure.
- **G3 Test-first:** every behaviour change has a unit test that fails before the fix and passes after
  (state the red→green evidence in the report). Never relax an assertion to go green.
- **G4 Live evidence:** at least one live arm that exercises the change (phy-test on sens6, OAI SA or OCUDU on
  sensnuc3), n ≥ 3 runs, alternated with the current `sdd/validation` baseline, reporting the per-run numbers
  (crc_ok/try, accepts by RNTI class, Technique D ARMED/CONVERGED, drop_full). Bar: fixed ≥ baseline and the
  targeted behaviour visibly happens (named log line). Rfsim/ZMQ 100 % CRC is an upper bound, not an OTA claim.
- **G5 Review:** an independent reviewer (fresh agent/session) reads the diff vs the lane base and returns
  "Approved"; Critical/Important findings fixed and re-reviewed.
- **G6 Hygiene:** `git add <explicit paths>` only (never `-A`), no stray files, no ground-truth seeding,
  commit message explains root cause + evidence, handover file updated.
- Merge into `sdd/validation` only after G1–G6; re-run G1+G2 on the merged tree; then one baseline live arm.
  `adaptive-rx-UL-DL` gets the merge only after the full matrix (section 4.4) is green.

### 4.1 OAI phy-test bed (sens6, no core)
- gNB: `/home/sens/NICOLA/agn-wt/gnbtest` (branch `sdd/rfsim-gnb-test` @ 67bb0eaa11, test-only, never merge),
  `nr-softmodem --phy-test --noS1 --rfsim`, virtual UE RNTI 0x1234, 106 PRB rank 1.
- Receiver: lane build of `nr-uesoftmodem --passive-rx --rfsim` with the fully agnostic 106 PRB conf used in
  phase A (exact command lines + scratch conf + `run_arm.sh` in `rfsim-results-phaseA.md` /
  `rfsim-results-val-phytest.md`). Multi-env values use `;` as separator in run_arm (the `,` bug shredded
  `ISAC_GNB_TEST_USS_AL=4,0,0,0,0`).
- Test-gNB knobs (env, default off): `ISAC_GNB_TEST_USS_AL=a1,a2,a4,a8,a16`, `ISAC_GNB_TEST_DL_DMRS_ID0/ID1`,
  `ISAC_GNB_TEST_DL_DATA_ID`, `ISAC_GNB_TEST_UL_DMRS_ID0/ID1/UL_DATA_ID`, `ISAC_GNB_TEST_DL_TYPEB_K0`,
  `ISAC_GNB_TEST_DL_TDA_IDX`, `ISAC_GNB_TEST_VRB_IL` (signalling only, no TX interleave),
  `ISAC_GNB_TEST_PRG` (n4 only, precoding wideband). Known test-gNB crashes: AL16 (AssertFatal phytest CCE),
  type-B k0=2 (segfault L1_tx_thread) → skip those arms here, use OCUDU.
- Score from receiver log: `accepts` by RNTI class, `pdsch_decode[try= crc_ok=]`, `Technique D ARMED/CONVERGED`,
  `scanq drop_full`, `DATA_ID_WALK`, al=, rnti=0x1234.
- Current baseline (sdd/validation, 150 s): crc_ok 0/3/1; A4 1/0/1; A2 1; A7 0 (oracle OK). TD never converges.

### 4.2 OAI SA (sensnuc3, core)
- `cd /home/sens/NICOLA/rfsim-val && ./tests/passive_rx/run_passive_rx.sh <secs> <outdir>` with env
  `CONF_TAG=.agn NUM_RX=1 NUM_UE=<1|3> RX1_NANT=4 TRAFFIC=udp IPERF_RATE=3M` (+ `ISAC_GNB_TEST_*`); rfsim-val's
  build dir has rfsim-local's test `nr-softmodem` symlinked in. Receiver conf `tests/passive_rx/ue.passive.agn.conf`
  (106 PRB, fully agnostic, `pdcch_blind_monitor_scan_thread` ON).
- Before each launch: `touch nr*_stats*.log; chmod 666 nr*_stats*.log` in the CWD (harness bug, lane misc fixes).
- Runs can overrun their duration (seen 30+ min for a 600 s run) — if > duration + 5 min, tear down your own
  run's processes (gNB last) and score the logs.
- B1 (UL scrambling IDs) is VOID on OAI: the override breaks the active OAI UE's uplink.
- Score C-RNTI-class accepts and C-RNTI PDSCH CRC — SI-RNTI SIB1 decodes do NOT validate the dedicated path.

### 4.3 OCUDU gNB via ZMQ (sensnuc3, core) — procedure being finalised in `ocudu-harness.md`
- Binaries: gNB `/home/sens/NICOLA/repos/ocudu/build/apps/gnb/gnb` (test knobs: branch `isac-test-knobs`,
  separate build — see `ocudu-test-knobs.md`), srsUE `/home/sens/NICOLA/repos/srs-ue/build/srsue/src/srsue`.
- **Co-start rule:** start the gNB first, wait for "Connection to AMF ... completed", then the broker/UEs. Never
  kill one side of a ZMQ link while the other runs (the survivor wedges at ~122 % CPU); restart all together.
- ZMQ is point-to-point, so a broker fans gNB DL to the active srsUE and to the passive OAI receiver
  (`--device.name oai_zmqdevif`), forwards srsUE UL to the gNB, and feeds the passive receiver DL+UL.
- Exact commands, sample rate/PRB choice, subscriber provisioning, receiver conf (`ue.passive.ocudu.conf`),
  start/stop script: see `ocudu-harness.md` (the harness agent writes it; copy the final procedure here).
- OCUDU YAML knobs for the matrix: see `ocudu-test-knobs.md` (search-space candidates per AL incl. AL1/AL16,
  MCS tables qam256/qam64LowSe, DM-RS type 2/additional positions, PDSCH/PUSCH TDA incl. type B, scrambling
  IDs, CSI-RS/TRS, retx forcing, multi-UE) + env knobs (RA type 0 DL/UL, VRB IL, BWP switch).

### 4.4 Final validation matrix (on the merged tree, each arm n ≥ 3, baseline alternated)
OCUDU: baseline; AL1-only; AL16; qam256; qam64LowSe; DM-RS type 2; DM-RS add-pos 1/2/3; PDSCH type B TDA;
PUSCH type B TDA; DL scrambling IDs; UL scrambling IDs; RA type 0 DL; RA type 0 UL; dynamicSwitch; VRB IL
n2/n4; BWP switch (if knob landed); forced retransmissions; 3 UEs; 2x2 (rank 2).
OAI phy-test: baseline; rank 4 (lane rank4). OAI SA: B0, B2, B4, B5.
Pass = the named receiver log line for the feature fires AND C-RNTI CRC > 0 (UL: PUSCH CRC > 0), no crash.

## 5. Takeover procedure (for an agent jumping in)

1. Read this file fully, then `progress.md` (last 40 lines) and the lane reports.
2. Check the lock `sensnuc3:/home/sens/NICOLA/AGENT_OWNER`. If owned by someone else and fresh (< 45 min): stop.
3. Quiescence check on BOTH hosts: `pgrep -af 'nr-uesoftmodem|nr-softmodem|srsue|run_passive_rx|lane-make|ninja|make'`
   and `pgrep -x gnb`. Also check each lane's last commit time (`git -C <worktree> log -1 --format=%ci`) and
   report mtime. A lane counts as abandoned only if no process, no commit, and no report update for > 45 min.
4. Write your lock line, append "TAKEOVER by <you> at <time>" to the Status log, mark lanes you adopt.
5. For each adopted lane: `git -C <worktree> status` — commit nothing you don't understand; finish, pass the
   gates (section 2), merge, update this file.
6. When stopping: update this file with exactly where you left off, release the lock
   (`echo "free <time> <note>" > AGENT_OWNER`).
